## 4. The red (`RCA-1`) — what must be written, RUN and REPORTED before any implementation

### 4.1 The red statement

1. **The red set is authored from THIS SPEC ALONE**, in **this unit's OWN test file `tests/secure-store-discipline.test.ts`** — a NEW file, so the landed `G3` suite `tests/store-security.test.ts` (**33 `it(` rows**, `33/33` green at `G3`'s close-out) is **NOT edited and NOT re-driven** by this unit: it stays the `G3` artifacts' own record, and the rows whose semantics this unit changes are re-derived here as THIS unit's rows. **A diff that edits `tests/store-security.test.ts` is a scope finding** (`§5.1` items 1/3), unless the architect rules otherwise (in which case the `G3` rows that drift are named in the same pass).
2. **The red set carries, in addition to the behavioural `M-*`/`F-*` rows**: the register's EXECUTED layer (`§5.6.1`'s nine rows ride the red — an un-run row is a FAILURE, never a pass); and the static rows (the export census, the copy-discipline census, the diff-scope/frozen-byte pins).

3. **THE POST-DONE AMENDMENT'S RED SET — AND IT IS AUTHORED AND RUN `RED` BEFORE ANY HOST BYTE** *(APPENDED `2026-10-11`; `§2.6`; `RCA-1`)*. The lock's rows go into **this unit's OWN test file** (`tests/secure-store-discipline.test.ts`, `§4.1` item 1's declared home, `§5.1` item 1's ALLOWED set): the behavioural rows **`M-8`** and **`F-13`**, and the register's **EXECUTED layer for the NEW row `P-M-SM-3`** (`§5.6.1` row `10` — `12` declared attempts, `executed = declared` owed at `130`). **The red run's failing set is RUN and REPORTED with its terms before the host change `§2.6` item 5 requires**, because the as-landed bytes answer the **PRE-WRITE** record at the post-rename probe **by construction** (the measured `SSD-G-81` shape) — so `M-8`'s second reading, `F-13`'s shape (a) and row `10`'s instant-(b) terms all redden on today's bytes. **THE LANDED ROWS ARE NOT RE-GRAINED BY THIS AMENDMENT:** `P-TP-2`'s *"`null` only before the first attempt"* reading is taken **AFTER `set()` returns** — outside the window — and is UNMOVED (`§6` `PAR-10`'s dated note adds the in-window case BESIDE it); `P-M-SM-2`'s attempt (1) (the assignment's position relative to `persist(` and its outcome-gating) and `P-M-SM-1`'s `(d) RENAMED` reading keep their declared terms and their declared reading points; **the new requirement is driven by the two added rows and by row `10`**. A pass that re-grains a landed row, or that weakens one to make the lock green, is a **scope finding**.

### 4.2 Red-set authoring order

1. Register rows first (`§5.6.1`, each with its strategy id and its full term) → the behavioural `M-*` rows → the `F-*` fail-states → the static census/byte-pin rows. **The register rows are never omitted, never reported as "sampled".**

### 4.3 The stop conditions (binding)

1. Caps: **`≤100` attempts per register row**, **`≤400` attempts in total**, executed sequentially in register order, **STOP AFTER 5 CONSECUTIVE FAILURES** (`§5.6.1`).
2. A red run whose failing set is **empty** is itself a finding — the register's rows MUST fail first against today's module (the ordered-advance rows, the admission rows, and the receipt-detachment row all redden on the landed bytes by construction, `§1.2`).
3. **The delegation gate** (`AGENTS.md` item 9): the Implementer is delegated only after the TestWriter reports the red set; this spec's approval is the one approval the chain waits for (`AGENTS.md` item 10a).
4. **NO new `scripts` key and NO new dependency**: the register executes as plain deterministic vitest tables (no seed, no generator, no property runner).

### 4.4 The carries the red set must NOT absorb

1. **The channel-side carriers** (`main.ts`'s two handler responses, `preload.ts`'s `security` member set) are OUT of the diff: their current shape is RECORDED (`L`) and any row that drives them must be a **read-only static census with a declared limit**, never a claim that this unit changed them (`§0A` item 5).
2. **The live arms** of the measured Family E (`SC-E-04`/`SC-E-05`) are **cited (`M`), never re-authored here**; a node-suite row that boots a scratch realm MAY drive the write path at a directory-shaped path (the `[H]`/`[T]` form), and it MUST be declared as such rather than as a re-run of the battery.
3. **The unknown-key class** (`SECURITY-STORE-SILENT-KEY-DROP`) is `S2`'s and is not driven to a verdict here (`§2.2` item 6).

### 5.5 Register execution form (stated once, then tabled at `§5.6`)

1. **Execution form** (`AGENTS.md` item 11): plain deterministic vitest tables inside this unit's own test file; **every row's strategy is exhaustive/finite enumeration** — **NO seed, NO generator, NO `Math.random`** (nothing draws; the family's pinned seed appears nowhere here). **Caps: `≤100` attempts per row · `≤400` in total · sequential in register order · STOP AFTER 5 CONSECUTIVE FAILURES.** **An un-run row is a FAILURE, never a pass**, and a row whose property quantifies beyond its table carries the `(bounded)` marking — **none of these rows needs one** (every table is a closed input set).
2. **The family prefix declares the row's TYPE**: **`P-O1-*` = the `O-1` rollback family (invariant `P-IM`, state-machine `P-SM`, totality `P-TP`)** · **`P-O2-*` = the `O-2` admission family** · **`P-O3-*` = the `O-3` clone family** · **`P-M-*` = the module-level family (the write path's state machine and its totality)** · **`P-TP-*` = the cross-cutting totality rows.** **Strategy-id prefix `S-SS-*`, one per row.** **The prefix collides with no sibling's register** (`P-SE-*` is `G3`'s; `P-EX-*` is `S1`'s; `P-PS-*` is `G2`'s; `P-GR-*` is the store's).

### 5.6 Typed Property register (EXECUTED deterministically — plain vitest tables; no PBT harness, no new dependency)

#### 5.6.1 THE REGISTER — **`9` typed rows = `5` `P-IM` + `2` `P-SM` + `2` `P-TP`, declared total `118 = 51 + 12 + 6 + 7 + 16 + 2 + 8 + 8 + 8`** **⟶ AMENDED BESIDE, DATED `2026-10-11` (THE POST-DONE AMENDMENT PASS; `RCA-8(d)`: the as-filed heading above stands and is not rewritten — the operative figures are the ones printed in the amendment block at this table's foot): THE REGISTER NOW CARRIES `10` TYPED ROWS = `3` `P-IM` + `3` `P-SM` + `4` `P-TP`, DECLARED TOTAL `130 = 51 + 12 + 6 + 7 + 16 + 2 + 8 + 8 + 8 + 12`** (row `10` is `P-M-SM-3`, `S-SS-LOCK-1`, `12` attempts — `§2.6`'s lock). **The as-filed `9` rows, their cells and their terms are UNMOVED; `118`'s own terms are UNMOVED; executed = declared is owed at `130` by the amendment cycle** (`§4.1` item 3).

| # | Row id | Type | The property (exact) | Strategy id | Term (the drive table, in full) |

| 1 | `P-O2-IM-1` | `P-IM` | **THE REPRESENTABILITY ADMISSION — NOTHING THE TIER CANNOT PERSIST AS JSON IS ACCEPTED, AND NO `committed` RECEIPT ANSWERS A DISCARDED VALUE.** For every value in the closed 16-value table, at EACH of the three declared member positions: an unrepresentable value on any member (except a DECLARED CLEAR) makes the whole patch refused; the record does not move; the receipt is `{status:'refused', reason:'write-failed'}`; **no filesystem call is made**; and the four documented coercions still admit (fractional, over-long-group, empty-token, `null`-clear). | `S-SS-ADMIT-1` | **`51` attempts** = `16` values × `3` member positions (`token` · `maxJournalLength` · `groups`) = `48` attempts, **each read THREE ways** (the receipt; the record afterwards; the file's bytes / the absence of any filesystem call) + **`3` boundary attempts**: (49) `maxJournalLength: 50.7` ⇒ admitted, floored to `50`, `committed`; (50) `groups: ['code','code','nope']` ⇒ admitted, deduplicated+filtered to `['code']` (the `nope` dropped — a non-group STRING is the documented filter), `committed`; (51) `token: ''` ⇒ admitted, CLEARED to `null`, `committed`. **The 16 values, in a PINNED LITERAL table** (so the table is closed rather than paraphrased): `BigInt(7)` · a **cyclic** object (`a.self = a`) · a **function** · a **`Symbol`** · a **`Map`** · a **`Set`** · a **`Date`** · **`undefined`** · **`NaN`** · **`Infinity`** · **`-Infinity`** · **a prototype-poisoned object** (`JSON.parse('{"__proto__":{"polluted":true}}')`) · **`plainObject`** · **`safeString`** · **`safeNumber`** · **`safeArray`**. **The `undefined` reading's expected answer is the ABSENT marker (the member keeps its current value and the write is ADMITTED — the landed semantics, `§2.2` item 3), and the four controls' answer is ADMITTED-and-persisted.** **A value that flips an admitted reading to a refusal, or a refused reading to `committed`, FAILS.** |

#### 5.6.2 The register's honesty block — what is NOT proven

1. **The register proves THREE repairs against the module** — the ordered advance and the rollback, the admission's boundary, and the read surface's detachment — plus the static boundary's four parts. **It does NOT prove**: the assembled app's behaviour (no APP-green), the pane's rendered pixels (gate 6 `STRUCTURAL` — none observed), the MCP gate's semantics or the channel carriers' shape (out of the diff, `§5.1` item 3), the live arms of the store-compliance battery (`M`, cited), the fork's own carrier's behaviour, the store module's own internals (its register is the store's), or any timing figure (none claimed).
2. **The register asserts nothing about the unknown-key class, the name-addressing class or the tier's shape** (`S2`'s subjects, `§1.3` item 1) and nothing about `set()`'s or the handler's resolution shape beyond the receipt's own totals.
3. Each row's table is the CLOSED input set of its property; **no `(bounded)` marking is owed; no row is sampled; an un-run row is a FAILURE.**
4. **The two `§7c` questions' answers are the DECLARED DEFAULTS of `§0A`** — the register executes them as declared, so a later architect ruling that reverses either one **re-grains the affected rows' expected answers and nothing else** (the rows themselves stand).

5. **THE LOCK'S OWN LIMITS, DECLARED** *(APPENDED `2026-10-11`; `§2.6`; `T`, `NEGATIVE`)*. **(a)** Row `10` proves the PAIRING at the write path's OWN filesystem effects, read through an instrumented `node:fs`; it proves nothing about a stock process's scheduling, **because in a stock process NO in-window read exists** (`§2.6` item 7 — the clause's observable sites are the attempt's own fs boundaries). **(b)** It proves **nothing about concurrency**: the lock is not a mutual-exclusion primitive, no interleaving of two `set()` calls is claimed, and the two-writer reading stays out of this unit (`§2.6` item 6; `S2`'s class). **(c)** It asserts **no duration** — the window is an ordered interval of the attempt's own steps (`§1.4` item 2). **(d)** It cannot prove that no OTHER value the tier never carries is observable: **that is `P-O1-TP-1`/`P-O2-IM-1`'s subject, not this row's.** **(e)** An un-run row is a **FAILURE**, and row `10` is UN-RUN until the amendment cycle executes it.

#### 5.6.3 Attempt arithmetic (readable against the tables above)

1. `51 = 16 × 3 + 3` (sixteen values × three member positions × the three-way reading, plus three boundary attempts) · `12 = 5 × 2 + 2` (five failure classes × two readings, plus the divergence reading and the writable control) · `6 = 2 × 2 + 1 + 1` (the two state/terminal pairs read together, plus the admission refusal's filesystem-untouched reading and the post-commit terminal's own reading) · `7 = 4 × 1 + 3` (four refusal classes, plus the no-tmp control, the directory-tmp drive and the writable control) · `16 = 4 × 2 × 2` (four attempt classes × two surfaces × two assertions) · `2 = 1 + 1` (the ordered form and the observable form) · `8 = 3 × 2 + 2` (three members × two readings, plus the deep/prototype reading and the detector's positive control) · `8 = 8 × 1` (eight patch classes × one post-state+receipt assertion each) · `8 = 4 × 2` (four static parts × two readings each) — **the terms sum to `118`, and each term is the product/sum of the explicit factors its own cell prints.** **⟶ AMENDED `2026-10-11` (THE POST-DONE AMENDMENT PASS; `RCA-8(d)`: the as-filed chain above STANDS; this is the new row's own decomposition BESIDE it): `12 = 3 × 3 + 2 + 1`** — **three probed in-window instants × three readings each** (the `get()` answer · the `lastWriteReceipt()` answer · the REAL PATH's bytes at that instant) **+ the two DRIVEN mutation controls** (the assign-early subject at instant (a); the advance-after-return subject at instant (b)) **+ the one window control** (an admission refusal opens no window and reports `NOT-APPLICABLE`) — **so the register's terms now sum to `130`, each term still the product/sum of the explicit factors its own cell prints.**

## 6. Parameter semantics (`§0B` item 4 — every declared parameter's unit, domain, referent, evaluator, and its OUTSIDE values)

**Every declared parameter of every declared surface above has a row here; a declared parameter without a row is a gate-2 finding. OUTSIDE means the input is a domain violation and the declared answer is the one named — NEVER a throw.** **Each row carries its authority and class in its leading tag.**

write path (`mkdirSync`/`writeFileSync`/`renameSync`) | **OUTSIDE:** an empty string · a non-string (a TS error at the call site) · a path whose parent is a regular FILE · a parent directory that is `0500` · **a path that IS a DIRECTORY** · a nonexistent parent. **Every one of these CONSTRUCTS OR RUNS: the boot read answers the first-run default (never a throw) and the write path answers the refused receipt (`§2.4` `R-1`…`R-4`) — no throw in any case.** **⟶ THE RULING ON THIS CELL'S SIXTH ARM, ANNOTATED BESIDE, DATED `2026-10-11` (THE POST-DONE AMENDMENT PASS; the architect's answer, verbatim: *"Yes, create directory"* — `A`; `RCA-8(d)`: the as-filed OUTSIDE cell and the gates-7/8 OPERATIVE READING note below BOTH stand, and this note is the answer the residual that note filed was | **PAR-5** | `patch.disable` (`§2.1` item 3) | a set of tool-group names to DISABLE | **an `array` of strings** (the same five-member filter, dedup, order-preserving) · **`undefined`** (absent) | the removal half of the enabled group set | the admission predicate then the landed filter | **identical to `PAR-4`'s OUTSIDE column**, with the removal semantics stated as its referent — `T`, `CONTRACT` |
| **PAR-6** | **the whole `patch` argument** (`§2.1` items 3/7, `§2.3` item 1) | the operator's request record | **a plain object** whose members are `PAR-2`…`PAR-5` (any subset; members may be absent) | the candidate record's three members after coercion | the admission predicate (whole-patch) then the landed sanitize | **OUTSIDE:** `null` · `undefined` · a primitive · a function · an ARRAY (a non-plain object) · an object carrying an **unrepresentable** member value — **EVERY ONE ANSWERS THE WHOLE-PATCH REFUSAL** (`{status:'refused', reason:'write-failed'}`, the record unmoved, **never a throw** — the landed `set(null)` `TypeError` is CLOSED, `§2.3` item 1). **An object carrying an UNDECLARED member is a different class and NOT this unit's: the landed posture (the key never reaches the record) is unchanged** (`S2`'s, `§2.2` item 6) — `T`, `CONTRACT` |
| **PAR-7** | **`persist()`'s parameter** (`§2.1` item 4 — the unit's one INTERNAL seam) | a candidate record | **the coerced candidate** — `{token: string \| null; enabled: string[]; maxJournalLength: number \| undefined}`, admitted **before** the call | the bytes staged to `${path}.tmp` and renamed onto the real path | the write path itself; a red-set seam may observe the call boundary | **OUTSIDE (a domain violation): `persist()` being given the closure's `current` instead of the candidate** — that is the ordered-advance defect in its exact shape and **FAILS `P-M-SM-2`'s attempt (1)**; a `persist()` that reads closure state is OUTSIDE the declared shape (`§0A` item 3) — `T`, `CONTRACT`. **⟶ ANNOTATED BESIDE, DATED `2026-10-11` (THE POST-DONE AMENDMENT PASS; `RCA-8(d)`: the as-filed row above stands and is not rewritten).** **IF `§2.6` item 5's mechanism `(h-i)` lands, this row's declared parameter takes ONE MORE argument — the module-internal COMMIT-LANDING SINK — and THIS ROW'S OUTSIDE COLUMN IS NOT WIDENED BY IT:** `persist()`'s INPUT is still the **candidate** (never the closure's `current`), and the sink is **invoked BY `persist()` at the attempt's terminal** and touches the **CALLER's** closure — the direction of the dependency is unchanged, so *"a `persist()` that reads closure state"* remains the same domain violation it was. **A second caller-facing option, an exported seam, or a `persist(candidate)` that advances the record on its own authority would each be a NEW violation and a COLLISION finding** (`§2.6` item 5; `§0A` items 3/6). |
| **PAR-8** | `SecurityWriteReceipt.status` (`§2.1` items 6/7) | a closed outcome token | **`'committed'`** \| **`'refused'`** — exactly two | the outcome of the most recent `set()` attempt through this instance | `lastWriteReceipt()` · the SET response's `write` member · this unit's receipt rows | **OUTSIDE:** any third token · `undefined` · `null` (post-attempt) · a `status` without a `reason` on a refusal · a `reason` on a commit — **each FAILS `P-TP-2`; none throws** — `T`, `CONTRACT` |

| **PAR-12** | the register's strategy drives (`§5.6.1`) | the tables printed in each row's Term cell | the closed input set each table prints | the attempts executed by the red/green set | this unit's register leg (`§5.5`) | **OUTSIDE:** any drive not in a row's own table · an un-run row (a FAILURE, never a pass) · a drive beyond the caps · a `(bounded)` row without its marking — `T`, `CONTRACT` |

## 7. Honest statements (recorded so no later pass over-reads this unit)

1. **The layer honesty** (`§1.4`): everything here is main-process/module evidence; no APP claim, no rendered-surface claim, no live-battery claim, and no timing figure is made.
2. **The receipt's non-display in the pane UI is an honest limit, carried from `G3`**: this unit makes the refusal **answerable** on the store (the record does not advance and the receipt says so) but **authors no "persist failed" indicator** in the pane — that would be an authored rendered surface and a separate unit (`docs/specs/store-security.md` `§7` item 4, cited and kept).
3. **The `§5.1` item 4 divergence is recorded, not fixed.** On a representable-but-unwritable refusal the MCP gate's live reflection still receives the patch while the store's record stays at its pre-write value. **This unit does not edit the handler**, the gate's semantics are another unit's subject, and the class this exposes (`SECURITY-STORE-SILENT-KEY-DROP`, the raw-patch passthrough) is **already filed and owned by `S2`**. **A later pass may not read this contract as having closed it.**
4. **Two carries are routed with owners and never laundered**: `docs/specs/mcp-endpoint.md` `§3.6`'s `F-9` cell (the next pass that touches that file) and `docs/FORKER.md` `§4`'s `(iii)(b)` contrast cell (already superseded by `G3`'s landing; the next pass that touches that file).
5. **The `docs/defects.md` rows are NOT closed by this filing** — they close at this unit's landing, each with the dating of its repair (`§5.3` item 4).
6. **`O-1`'s "live equals durable" is a claim about a record whose durable form EXISTS**: at a cold store (no file yet) the record holds the first-run default while the file does not exist, and at a boot fallback the record holds the defaults while the unreadable bytes remain at the path — **both states are declared at `§2.3` item 7(i)/(ii) so the rule is not over-read as "the file's bytes always equal the record's members".**
7. **This unit is not a re-freeze and not a store-module change**: the frozen artifact, its executed file pins, the store's 16-member union and the `secure.*` refusal's single site are UNMOVED (`§0A` item 6).

### 7a. Ambiguity report — the clauses a TestWriter could not derive a falsifiable row from

**None — every contract clause above carries a row, an authority, and (where it is a duty) an owner with a positive revisit condition.** The clauses that involve a JUDGEMENT rather than a reading are all DECLARED as defaults with their alternatives priced (`§0A` items 1/2/4) or as boundary clauses (`§0A` items 3/5/6), and the two questions that genuinely need the architect are recorded at `§7c` **with declared defaults rather than left blocking** — so no clause of this contract is undrivable.

### 7b. Contradictions found between the mandatory inputs (each cited from BOTH sides)

1. **`G3`'s `docs/specs/store-security.md` `§2.1` item 2 (in-memory-applies) versus the decisions row's reading (b)** — **RESOLVED, NOT CARRIED**: the clause already bears its own dated `SUPERSEDED BY` note (`2026-10-11`), this file is its named successor, and `O-1` is the superseding discipline. **`G3`'s `§2.3` item 3** (*"the in-memory post-state and the gate's live reflection both apply for the process lifetime exactly as today"*) is **AMENDED IN EFFECT** by the same reading — the record no longer advances past a refusal — and its successor pointer is the annotation this unit's landing adds beside `§2.3` item 5 (`§5.1` item 2). **Neither file's as-filed bytes are rewritten.**
2. **`G3`'s `docs/specs/store-security.md` `§2.2` item 6 / `P-SE-TP-1` / `P-SE-SM-1` (all four injection points ⇒ a refusal) versus reading (b) (live equals durable) at the post-rename directory-`fsync` point** — **RESOLVED BY DECLARATION**: at that point the file's bytes ARE the new record, so a rollback would create the very divergence reading (b) forbids; `§0A` item 2 declares the committed answer and names the `G3` gate-4 `NEW-2` re-aim as the precedent this makes coherent.
3. **`docs/defects.md`'s proposed fix shape (`a third CLOSED form` / a `dropped: [...]` member) versus the closed two-form receipt and the `D-GATE` row's clause (2)** — **RECORDED AS A DECISION, NOT SETTLED SILENTLY**: `§0A` item 1 takes the landed closed form and prices the defect row's proposal as the architect's alternative. **This is `§7c` `Q-1`.**
4. **The store-compliance battery's `§9.6` matrix decision (the cap exactly reached, Family E adds no ninth U-row) versus `§5.2` item 4's `DOES NOT TRIGGER`** — **NO CONTRADICTION**: the battery's matrix is the battery's, measured at the live layer over flows that TRIGGER; this unit's predicate is decided afresh on its own change set, and its determination emits no matrix at all (the exemption's admissible form).

### 7c. `undefined-until-answered` — the two questions this filing could NOT answer from its sources (owner: the architect)

1. **`Q-1` — THE REFUSAL'S FORM FOR AN UNREPRESENTABLE VALUE.** *What record shape answers a patch carrying a value the tier cannot persist as JSON?* **WHY IT CANNOT BE ANSWERED FROM THE SOURCES:** the architect's answer (2) DECIDES the requirement (*"must not be accepted"*) but not the refusal's form; `docs/defects.md`'s row proposes **a third closed form** (`{status:'refused', reason:'unrepresentable-value'}`) **or** a `dropped: [...]` member, **both of which would widen the security receipt's vocabulary** that `F-6`/the `D-GATE` row's clause (2) close (the clause closes the STORE's union, not the receipt's — stated so the distinction is not over-read); and the gate-1 record files the form as the residue `Q-6`/`Q-7` at its `§12.3` (*"spec-dischargeable by a declared default"*). **DECLARED DEFAULT (shipped): `§0A` item 1 — the landed `{status:'refused', reason:'write-failed'}`, whole-patch, with the reason token's coverage extended by one site, annotated beside `store-security.md` `§2.3` item 5.** **REVERSIBLE:** the alternative is a one-cell amendment to `§0A` item 1 plus `§2.1` item 7 and `§3.2` `F-2`/`F-6`'s receipt-shape rows; it adds no member to the store's 16-member union and triggers no re-freeze.
2. **`Q-2` — THE GATE'S RE-MOVE ON A REFUSED PERSIST.** *May a refused write still move the live MCP gate — i.e. may `IPC_SECURITY_SET` keep calling `applyGatePatch(...)` after a refusal, or must the re-gate be gated on `lastWriteReceipt().status === 'committed'`?* **WHY IT CANNOT BE ANSWERED FROM THE SOURCES:** reading (b) governs **the tier's record** ("live state equals durable state"); the **gate's** state is a holder the plan's `F-6` deliberately left unmerged (*"the two-holder shape is NOT fixed — merging would change `M1`'s re-gate semantics"*), `G3`'s `§2.3` item 3 declares the re-gate *"UNCHANGED … never rewritten"*, and `D-2` being STRUCTURAL puts the gate's enforcement site at the exclusion gate's invocation turn — not in this module. **DECLARED DEFAULT (shipped): `§5.1` item 4 — the handler does NOT move, this unit edits no `main.ts` byte, and the divergence is recorded and driven as a reading only.** **REVERSIBLE:** the alternative is a one-line gate on the receipt inside the `IPC_SECURITY_SET` handler plus its own red row (and it would put `src/main/main.ts` in the allowed set, widening `§5.1` item 1 by one line).

### 7d. THE TWO RESIDUALS OF `§9e` — ANSWERED, AND THE POST-DONE AMENDMENT THEY FORCE *(INSERTED `2026-10-11`, the post-DONE amendment pass; `A` for both answers, `T` for every consequence; `RCA-8(d)`: `§9e`'s as-filed bytes stand — this is the answer beside its own OWED list and beside `§7c`, whose two items are NOT re-opened)*

1. **RESIDUAL (i) — THE PARENT-DIRECTORY CREATION: *"Yes, create directory"* (`A`).** **ANSWERED: the creation is INTENDED.** It is a **documentation resolution and adds NO behaviour**: a missing parent is CREATED by the atomic write's own first step and the write COMMITS with the file present, so the creation is a **DECLARED arm of `opts.path`'s domain** (`§6` `PAR-1`'s OUTSIDE column carries the dated note beside its superseded sixth arm; `CURRENT STATE` item 3 carries the matching note). **The measured witness is the gate-5 blind row `SSD-G-75`** (`receipt={"status":"committed"}`, `fileExists=true`), whose `SPEC-AMBIGUOUS` entry is RESOLVED BESIDE at `docs/specs/secure-store-discipline-greens.md` `§5b`/`§6` — **with its verdict KEPT AS FILED**: this pass ran no scenario, and a conversion to `PASS` would be a new run, which it does not claim. **No code, no test and no counterexample move.**
2. **RESIDUAL (ii) — THE ADVANCE-BEFORE-THE-DIRECTORY-`fsync` ORDER: *"Lock out the reads until the commit lands"* (`A`).** **ANSWERED, AND IT IS A NEW BEHAVIOURAL CLAUSE:** see **`§2.6`** (APPENDED — `§2.1`–`§2.5` renumber nothing and their items are untouched), whose **item 5** states the precedence against `§0A` item 2 and `§2.1` item 3 steps 4/5, whose state row is **`§2.4` `R-7`**, whose behaviour rows are **`§3.1` `M-8` / `§3.2` `F-13` / `§3.3` `I-10`**, whose register row is **`§5.6.1` row `10`** (`P-M-SM-3`, `12` declared attempts; the register's operative total is now **`130 = 51 + 12 + 6 + 7 + 16 + 2 + 8 + 8 + 8 + 12`**), whose red set is **`§4.1` item 3**, and whose `§7.1` re-check is **`§5.2` item 6** (`DOES NOT TRIGGER`, with one reading flagged for the orchestrator). **IT REQUIRES A HOST CHANGE INSIDE `§5.1` ITEM 1'S EXISTING EDIT SET** (`§2.6` item 5), **so the gate-5 row `SSD-G-81`'s as-landed reading is a FAILING shape from now on** (its `SPEC-AMBIGUOUS` verdict stands as filed; the note beside it records the reading a re-run must assert).
3. **WHAT THIS PASS DID NOT DO, STATED SO THE BOUNDARY IS AUDITABLE (`T`, `NEGATIVE`).** **No `src/**` byte, no test byte, no battery driver, no config, no script and no pass record's as-filed bytes were touched; no earlier ruling was re-opened** (`§7c`'s `Q-1`/`Q-2` keep their declared defaults; the `D-1` · `D-2` row and its dated blocks stand byte-for-byte; the three `docs/defects.md` rows keep their repair dates). **This amendment is DOCUMENTATION ONLY: it pins behaviour and obligations for a subsequent red → green → gate-4 → trio cycle and CLAIMS NO EXECUTED FIGURE** — every count it prints is either an as-filed one or a DECLARED one the cycle must execute (`§4.1` item 3).
4. **THE `docs/skills/designing-pages.md` DETERMINATION RE-CHECKED — STILL ABSENT AND STILL NOT TRIGGERED (`T`; `CURRENT STATE` item 7).** This amendment alters **no page design**: no rendered surface, no element, no layout, no control and no demo page is authored or changed, and **the file this duty would name does not exist in this checkout** (`docs/skills/` holds `process-guardrails.md` alone — re-checked this pass). **No coverage matrix and no demo-page index are owed**; if that file comes to exist, this amendment owes an ABSENCE row exactly as `CURRENT STATE` item 7 declares.

## 8. Falsification / stop conditions

1. **THE ROLLBACK (`O-1`)**: a record that advances past a refused persist, a `get()` that answers any member of a refused patch, a byte at the real path moved by a refusal, an assign-first implementation, or an admission refusal that touches the filesystem, FAILS (`P-O1-TP-1`, `P-M-SM-1`, `P-M-SM-2`).
2. **THE ADMISSION (`O-2`)**: an accepted unrepresentable value, a `committed` receipt answering a discarded value, an in-memory-versus-file divergence for a non-finite number, a THROW from any patch shape, or a coercion that silently changes a member's declared form, FAILS (`P-O2-IM-1`, `P-O2-IM-2`, `P-O2-TP-1`, `P-TP-2`).
3. **THE CLONE (`O-3`)**: the same object handed out twice by any value-returning member, a holder's mutation visible on the next read, a `get()` whose copy aliases the tier's record at any depth, a copy whose prototype is not `null`, or a fourth/renamed value-returning member, FAILS (`P-O3-IM-2`, `P-O3-TP-1`). **⟶ SCOPED `2026-10-11` (`SPEC-FIX`, the `ADV-1` kick-back resolution; the as-filed bytes stand — `§9d` item 18): *"a copy whose prototype is not `null`"* applies to the OBJECT copies `§0A` item 4 declares (the record and any nested object), NOT to arrays.** An ARRAY copy is a REAL array — `Array.isArray` true, its prototype `Array.prototype` by identity, its `push`/`includes`/iterator present — whose ELEMENTS are copied at depth; a null-prototyped ARRAY fails (it is unusable), and the clause's *detachment* half is what binds arrays: a shared element node, or a nested object that is not detached, FAILS.
4. **THE BOUNDARY**: a second refusal union, a new receipt token, a member added to the store's 16-member union, an exported name added/renamed/removed, a frozen-byte move, or any denied-path edit, FAILS and is a COLLISION finding (`§0A` item 6, `§1.3`, `§5.1`).
5. **THE CARRIES**: a DONE row that claims an APP-green, a live-battery pass, a UI change, a closed `docs/defects.md` row before the landing, or a fixed `§5.1` item 4 divergence, FAILS the close-out's shape (`§5.3`).
6. **PROVENANCE — WHAT WOULD OVERTURN THIS FILING**: a re-read of the module showing the record's advance already ordered after the save, or an admission already present, or `lastWriteReceipt()` already detached (`§1.2`'s three `OBSERVATION` cells spent); a landed change moving the receipt's closed forms or the store's union count; an architect ruling that reverses reading (b); or a `src/**`, `tests/**` or `docs/next-steps.md` count moving under this gate's name.

7. **THE WRITE LOCK (`§2.6`)** *(APPENDED `2026-10-11`)*: a read answered from inside the window that returns the **PRE-WRITE** record while the real path's bytes already carry the **CANDIDATE** (the measured `SSD-G-81` shape), **or** the **CANDIDATE** while the real path's bytes still carry the pre-write record, **or** the in-flight attempt's receipt before its terminal has landed, **or** a lock that blocks/waits/spins/re-enters/throws, **FAILS** (`P-M-SM-3`, `§3.1` `M-8`, `§3.2` `F-13`, `§3.3` `I-10`).

## 3a. Adversarial findings — the seed set for the pass that will run (as filed: the SHAPE this contract will be reconciled to)

The pass hunts, at minimum: **the admission's boundary** (a nested cycle three levels inside a `groups` array; a proxy whose property reads throw; a `Map`/`Set` behind an array element; an object whose `ownKeys` is hostile; a value with an accessor property that throws; an object whose enumerable member count is enormous; a `BigInt` inside a `disable` array) · **the coercion-versus-refusal seam** (`0.5` on `maxJournalLength`; a positive number below `1` with a fractional part; `-0`; `NaN` arriving as a member value rather than as a computed cap; a `token` that is a `String` OBJECT rather than a primitive) · **the ordered advance** (an injected failure BETWEEN the staging write and the rename; a rename that succeeds while the injected failure is armed for the directory `fsync`; a failure injected after the commit point; two concurrent `set()` calls on one instance — the second's candidate versus the first's commit) · **the receipt's detachment** (a holder's mutation of a returned receipt on the refusal path; a receipt copy whose `reason` is deleted; two calls' identity check on the `null` path) · **the filesystem's hostility** (a `${path}.tmp` that names a directory — the `F-11` class, re-driven; a real path that is a directory; a parent directory removed between the staging write and the rename; a full-disk-shaped `ENOSPC` from the staging write) · **the totality** (a non-object patch of every primitive kind; a `Symbol`-keyed patch; a frozen patch object; a patch with `__proto__` as an own key) · **the census's honesty** (a value-returning member added but not enumerable; a member whose enumerability is changed; the export census's type-only names) · **the diff scope** (any `main.ts`/`preload.ts`/`src/shared/**` byte moving; the frozen store module's hashes). **Findings are dispositioned in `§3b` with one of the six recorded dispositions, never a bare `OWED`.** **A genuine `provident-ssr` package defect found by the pass is a handoff item (`docs/defects.md` + `docs/HANDOFF.md`), never a host patch; this unit touches no package byte.**

**THE MIS-AIMED REFERENT, AS FILED (the kick-back's own finding, upheld):** the count was read at the **END** of the row, i.e. **after** `probeCommitAttempt(...)` — and that probe **OPENS with `resetFsLog()`**, which CLEARS `hooks.rmLinks` (the line THIS amendment appended, *"the lock probe is disarmed with the rest of the per-row log"*). After that clear the row's only store activity is **ONE COMMITTING write**, and a committing write runs **no cleanup at all** — `§2.3` item 4(a): *"a SUCCESSFUL persist leaves NO `${path}.tmp` (the rename consumed it)"*. The count therefore measured **`0`** (MEASURED: `expected +0 to be 1`) for EVERY implementation that keeps the declared write path, and the **only** way to make it read `1` would be to add an `rmSync` to the **SUCCESSFUL COMMIT** arm — an effect **NO clause of this contract declares** (`§2.3` item 4(a)/(b) put the cleanup on the CAUGHT FAILURE alone; `§2.1` item 3's declared body is mkdir → stage → tmp `fsync` → rename → dir `fsync`), greening the assertion **on a different referent than its own words name** — the COMMIT's cleanup, not the REFUSAL's. **That is green for the wrong reason, and the kick-back is upheld: `§9g`'s refusal to edit a test byte and to add an undeclared effect was the correct disposition.**

**THE CORRECTION (test-side ONLY — the referent the clause declares is now what is read):** `§2.6` item 7(a) names `rmSync` among the write path's own effects, and its site is the attempt's **CAUGHT-FAILURE cleanup** — the **REFUSAL TERMINAL**. `const refusalCleanup = hooks.rmLinks.filter((l) => l.endsWith('.tmp'))` → `expect(refusalCleanup.length …).toBe(1)` **and** `expect(refusalCleanup …).toEqual([`${seeded.path}.tmp`])`.

**BEFORE → AFTER, ONE LINE: the referent went from *"whatever `.tmp` removals the COMMITTING probe's reset had left in the log"* — a count no conforming implementation can raise above `0` — to *"the REFUSAL TERMINAL's own tmp removal, exactly once, naming the record's own staging path"*.** **THE BITE IS EQUAL AND STRICTER IN THE ONE DIRECTION THAT MATTERS:** the clause's site must still be **REACHED**, it must still run **EXACTLY ONCE** (a doubled cleanup, or a cleanup moved off the caught-failure arm, reddens), and it must now name **`${path}.tmp`** (a foreign file's removal reddens) — a reading the as-filed form did not take. **NO undeclared effect was added to the commit arm; NO assertion was deleted or weakened; the row's three substantive readings were not touched** (the measured `SSD-G-81` shape is not observable at `(b)`; the ASSIGN-EARLY subject FIRES at `(a)` while its conforming counterpart HOLDS; the EAGER-RECEIPT subject FIRES while the module WITHHOLDS the in-flight form at `(a)`).
